(and a vagrant actually in the riparian)----Day notes: On this day, we encountered the most incredible wave of Eastern passerine vagrants I have personally ever experienced in Baja. The combination of overcast, light winds (the ideal weather for the Baja north coast but seemingly not farther south), and incredible reports from San Diego and offshore Baja had us all excited for the prospects of today--and it panned out! There were vagrants EVERYWHERE! Not only a great introduction to Baja for CWH and AWH, but started the motto of "I've never seen anything here" leading to a vagrant that held through the entire trip.

Exotics (annoyingly) persists, though (luckily) the species don't seem to have yet taken hold in ensenada. Very happy to finally encounter two true vagrants here on the same visit, plus quite a few very good birds that were not traditional vagrants!----Day notes: On this day, we encountered the most incredible wave of Eastern passerine vagrants I have personally ever experienced in Baja. The combination of overcast, light winds (the ideal weather for the Baja north coast but seemingly not farther south), and incredible reports from San Diego and offshore Baja had us all excited for the prospects of today--and it panned out! There were vagrants EVERYWHERE! Not only a great introduction to Baja for CWH and AWH, but started the motto of "I've never seen anything here" leading to a vagrant that held through the entire trip.
